+void RecipeCacheLRU::UpdateCachePath(const std::string& new_path) {
+
+  std::vector<std::string> split_config = serialization::RecipeCacheConfig::split_params(GET_ENV_FLAG_NEW(PT_HPU_RECIPE_CACHE_CONFIG));
+  split_config[0] = new_path;
+  std::ostringstream oss;
+  std::copy(split_config.begin(), split_config.end(), std::ostream_iterator<std::string>(oss, ","));
+  std::string new_config = oss.str();
+  new_config = new_config.empty() ? new_config : new_config.substr(0, new_config.length() - 1);
+
+  SET_ENV_FLAG_NEW(PT_HPU_RECIPE_CACHE_CONFIG, new_config.c_str(), 1);
+  recipe_cache_config_.reload();
+}
